1	# Logo Ideas
2	
3	Generate a grid of distinct logo concepts from a single brief.
4	
5	Pipeline:
6	1. **Concepts** — call Kimi K2 on Fireworks (`accounts/fireworks/models/kimi-k2p5` by default) to expand the brief into N varied logo prompts.
7	2. **Renders** — call fal.ai with one prompt per image. Default provider is `z-image` (`fal-ai/z-image/turbo`, 1 image per call). Pass `--provider gpt-image` to use `openai/gpt-image-2` instead.
8	3. **Grid** — assemble a contact sheet of all renders for review.
9	
10	## Inputs
11	
12	- `--ideas` *(required)* — free-form brief: brand, vibe, motifs, constraints.
13	- `--count` — number of logos to generate. Default `9`.
14	- `--out` *(required)* — output directory. Concepts, prompts, images, and the grid land here.
15	- `--provider` — `z-image` (default) or `gpt-image`.
16	- `--model` — override the Fireworks chat model id.
17	- `--image-size` — fal image size preset or `WIDTHxHEIGHT`. Default `square_hd`.
18	- `--env-file` — env file containing `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` and `FAL_KEY`.
19	- `--dry-run` — write the plan, skip both API calls.
20	
21	## Env
22	
23	- `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` — required for the concept step.
24	- `FAL_KEY` — required for the render step.
25	
26	Both are looked up from the `--env-file`, `this.env`, `.env`, or the standard workspace fallbacks.
27	
28	## Outputs
29	
30	```
31	{out}/
32	  logo-plan.json
33	  concepts.json          # raw concepts from Kimi (prompt, name, rationale)
34	  prompts.json           # final prompts sent to fal
35	  images/logo-001.png ...
36	  grid.jpg               # contact sheet of all renders
37	  logo-manifest.json     # per-candidate result with image paths
38	```
39
```

> AGENT

Yes — `builtin.logo_ideas` is the orchestrator. Quick summary:

**Pipeline** (`artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/`)
1. Kimi K2 (Fireworks) drafts N varied logo prompts from your brief
2. fal.ai renders each — Z-Image Turbo by default, or GPT Image 2 via `--provider gpt-image`
3. Assembles a contact-sheet grid

**Key flags:** `--ideas` (brief), `--count` (default 9), `--out`, `--provider`, `--image-size`, `--dry-run`

**Env:** `FIREWORKS_API_KEY`, `FAL_KEY`

Outputs land in `{out}/` as `logo-plan.json`, `concepts.json`, `prompts.json`, `images/logo-NNN.png`, `grid.jpg`, `logo-manifest.json`. Prior runs are visible in `runs/artagents_logo*`.

45	
46	def _load_env_var(name: str, env_file: Path | None) -> str:
47	    import os
48	
49	    value = os.environ.get(name, "").strip()
50	    if value:
51	        return value
52	    tried: list[str] = [f"{name} environment variable"]
53	    for candidate in _candidate_env_files(env_file):
54	        tried.append(str(candidate))
55	        if value := _read_env_value(candidate, name):
56	            return value
57	    raise SystemExit(f"{name} not found. Tried: {', '.join(tried)}")
58	
59	
60	def write_json(path: Path, payload: Any) -> None:
61	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
62	    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
63	
64	
65	def parse_image_size(value: str) -> str | dict[str, int]:
66	    if value in FAL_PRESETS:
67	        return value
68	    match = re.fullmatch(r"([1-9][0-9]*)x([1-9][0-9]*)", value.strip())
69	    if not match:
70	        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(
71	            "image-size must be a fal preset (square_hd, portrait_16_9, ...) or WIDTHxHEIGHT"
72	        )
73	    return {"width": int(match.group(1)), "height": int(match.group(2))}
74	
75	
76	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
77	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate a grid of logo ideas via Kimi K2 + fal.")
78	    parser.add_argument("--ideas", required=True, help="Brief describing the logo: brand, vibe, motifs, constraints.")
79	    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="Output directory for concepts, prompts, images, and the grid.")

85	        help=f"Image provider (default {DEFAULT_PROVIDER}).",

87	    parser.add_argument(

90	        help=f"Fireworks chat model id (default {DEFAULT_FIREWORKS_MODEL}).",

111	        parser.error("--count must be >= 1")

116	def build_layout(out_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
117	    root = out_dir.expanduser().resolve()
118	    layout = {
119	        "root": root,
120	        "images": root / "images",
121	    }

127	def _http_post_json(url: str, headers: dict[str, str], payload: dict[str, Any], *, timeout: int = 120) -> dict[str, Any]:
128	    body = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
129	    request = Request(url, data=body, method="POST")
130	    request.add_header("content-type", "application/json")
131	    for key, value in headers.items():
132	        request.add_header(key, value)
133	    with urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
134	        raw = response.read()
135	    return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")) if raw else {}
136	
137	
138	def _http_get_json(url: str, headers: dict[str, str], *, timeout: int = 60) -> dict[str, Any]:
139	    request = Request(url, method="GET")
140	    for key, value in headers.items():
141	        request.add_header(key, value)
142	    with urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
143	        raw = response.read()
144	    return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")) if raw else {}
145	
146	
147	def _http_get_bytes(url: str, *, timeout: int = 120) -> bytes:
148	    with urlopen(Request(url, method="GET"), timeout=timeout) as response:
149	        return response.read()
150	
151	
152	def _system_prompt() -> str:
153	    return (
154	        "You are a senior brand designer. Given a logo brief, propose distinct, "
155	        "creatively varied logo concepts. Each concept should differ in style, "
156	        "composition, or motif — not minor color variants. Return only JSON."
157	    )
158	
159	
160	def _user_prompt(ideas: str, count: int) -> str:
161	    return (
162	        f"Brief:\n{ideas}\n\n"
163	        f"Produce exactly {count} logo concepts. Return JSON of the shape:\n"
164	        '{"concepts":[{"name":"short title","rationale":"1 sentence why",'
165	        '"prompt":"single self-contained image-gen prompt, ~40 words, '
166	        "describing style, layout, palette, typography hints, no negative prompts, "
167	        'no text like \\"logo:\\""}]}\n'
168	        "The 'prompt' field is fed verbatim to a text-to-image model — make it "
169	        "concrete and visual. Avoid trademarked references unless the brief asks."
170	    )
171	
172	
173	def call_fireworks_concepts(
174	    *,
175	    ideas: str,
176	    count: int,
177	    model: str,
178	    api_key: str,
179	) -> dict[str, Any]:
180	    payload = {
181	        "model": model,
182	        "messages": [
183	            {"role": "system", "content": _system_prompt()},
184	            {"role": "user", "content": _user_prompt(ideas, count)},
185	        ],
186	        "temperature": 0.9,
187	        "max_tokens": 4000,
188	        "response_format": {"type": "json_object"},
189	    }
190	    headers = {"authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"}
191	    try:
192	        response = _http_post_json(FIREWORKS_CHAT_URL, headers, payload, timeout=180)
193	    except HTTPError as exc:
194	        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
195	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks chat call failed ({exc.code}): {detail}") from exc
196	    except URLError as exc:
197	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks chat call failed: {exc}") from exc
198	    return response
199	
200	
201	def parse_concepts(response: dict[str, Any], *, count: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
202	    choices = response.get("choices") or []
203	    if not choices:
204	        raise SystemExit("Fireworks response had no choices")
205	    content = (choices[0].get("message") or {}).get("content") or ""
206	    text = content.strip()
207	    try:
208	        data = json.loads(text)
209	    except json.JSONDecodeError:
210	        match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
211	        if not match:
212	            raise SystemExit(f"Could not parse JSON from Fireworks response: {text[:300]}")
213	        data = json.loads(match.group(0))
214	    raw_concepts = data.get("concepts") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
215	    if not isinstance(raw_concepts, list) or not raw_concepts:
216	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks response missing 'concepts' list: {text[:300]}")
217	    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
218	    for index, item in enumerate(raw_concepts[:count], start=1):
219	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
220	            continue
221	        prompt = str(item.get("prompt") or "").strip()
222	        if not prompt:
223	            continue
224	        concepts.append(
225	            {
226	                "candidate_id": f"logo-{index:03d}",
227	                "index": index,
228	                "name": str(item.get("name") or f"Concept {index}").strip(),
229	                "rationale": str(item.get("rationale") or "").strip(),
230	                "prompt": prompt,
231	            }
232	        )
233	    if not concepts:
234	        raise SystemExit("Fireworks response yielded no usable concepts")
235	    return concepts
236	
237	
238	def _planned_concepts(ideas: str, count: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
239	    return [
240	        {
241	            "candidate_id": f"logo-{index:03d}",
242	            "index": index,
243	            "name": f"Planned concept {index}",
244	            "rationale": "Dry-run placeholder; no model call was made.",
245	            "prompt": f"[dry-run] minimalist logo for: {ideas}",
246	        }
247	        for index in range(1, count + 1)
248	    ]
249	
250	
251	def _fal_payload(provider: str, prompt: str, image_size: str | dict[str, int], output_format: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
252	    fmt = "jpeg" if output_format == "jpg" else output_format
253	    if provider == "z-image":
254	        return {
255	            "prompt": prompt,
256	            "image_size": image_size,
257	            "num_images": 1,
258	            "output_format": fmt,
259	        }
260	    if provider == "gpt-image":
261	        return {
262	            "prompt": prompt,
263	            "image_size": image_size,
264	            "num_images": 1,
265	            "output_format": fmt,
266	            "quality": "high",
267	        }
268	    raise SystemExit(f"unknown provider {provider!r}")
269	
270	
271	def submit_fal_job(provider: str, payload: dict[str, Any], api_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
272	    model_id = PROVIDER_MODEL_IDS[provider]
273	    url = f"{FAL_QUEUE_URL}/{model_id}"
274	    headers = {"authorization": f"Key {api_key}"}
275	    try:
276	        return _http_post_json(url, headers, payload, timeout=120)
277	    except HTTPError as exc:
278	        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
279	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submit failed ({exc.code}) for {model_id}: {detail}") from exc
280	    except URLError as exc:
281	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submit failed for {model_id}: {exc}") from exc
282	
283	
284	def poll_fal_result(submission: dict[str, Any], api_key: str, *, max_wait_sec: int = 300) -> dict[str, Any]:
285	    status_url = submission.get("status_url")
286	    response_url = submission.get("response_url")
287	    if not status_url or not response_url:
288	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submission missing status_url/response_url: {submission}")
289	    headers = {"authorization": f"Key {api_key}"}
290	    deadline = time.monotonic() + max_wait_sec
291	    delay = 2.0
292	    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
293	        try:
294	            status = _http_get_json(status_url, headers, timeout=30)
295	        except HTTPError as exc:
296	            detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
297	            raise SystemExit(f"fal status poll failed ({exc.code}): {detail}") from exc
298	        state = str(status.get("status") or "").upper()
299	        if state in {"COMPLETED", "OK"}:
300	            return _http_get_json(response_url, headers, timeout=60)
301	        if state in {"FAILED", "ERROR", "CANCELLED"}:
302	            raise SystemExit(f"fal job {state}: {status}")
303	        time.sleep(delay)
304	        delay = min(delay * 1.4, 8.0)
305	    raise SystemExit(f"fal job timed out after {max_wait_sec}s; last status_url={status_url}")
306	
307	
308	def _ext_for_format(output_format: str) -> str:
309	    return "jpg" if output_format in ("jpeg", "jpg") else output_format
310	
311	
312	def _save_first_image(result: dict[str, Any], dest: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
313	    images = result.get("images") or []
314	    if not images:
315	        raise SystemExit(f"fal result had no images: {result}")
316	    first = images[0]
317	    url = first.get("url")
318	    if not url:
319	        raise SystemExit(f"fal image entry missing url: {first}")
320	    data = _http_get_bytes(url, timeout=180)
321	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
322	    dest.write_bytes(data)
323	    return {
324	        "path": str(dest),
325	        "source_url": url,
326	        "width": first.get("width"),
327	        "height": first.get("height"),
328	        "content_type": first.get("content_type"),
329	        "bytes": len(data),
330	    }
331	
332	
333	def _placeholder_image(dest: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
334	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
335	    try:
336	        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
337	    except ImportError:
338	        dest.write_bytes(b"")
339	        return {"path": str(dest), "placeholder": True, "reason": "Pillow unavailable"}
340	    image = Image.new("RGB", (768, 768), (24, 24, 28))
341	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
342	    try:
343	        font = ImageFont.truetype("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial Bold.ttf", 28)
344	    except OSError:
345	        font = ImageFont.load_default()
346	    draw.text((24, 24), "[dry-run]", fill=(220, 220, 220), font=font)
347	    draw.text((24, 72), label[:48], fill=(180, 180, 180), font=font)
348	    image.save(dest, quality=90)
349	    return {"path": str(dest), "placeholder": True, "reason": "dry-run"}
350	
351	
352	def render_concepts(
353	    *,
354	    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]],
355	    layout: dict[str, Path],
356	    provider: str,
357	    image_size: str | dict[str, int],
358	    output_format: str,
359	    fal_key: str | None,
360	    dry_run: bool,
361	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
362	    ext = _ext_for_format(output_format)
363	    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
364	    for concept in concepts:
365	        candidate_id = concept["candidate_id"]
366	        dest = layout["images"] / f"{candidate_id}.{ext}"
367	        if dry_run or not fal_key:
368	            generated = _placeholder_image(dest, concept["name"])
369	        else:
370	            payload = _fal_payload(provider, concept["prompt"], image_size, output_format)
371	            submission = submit_fal_job(provider, payload, fal_key)
372	            result = poll_fal_result(submission, fal_key)
373	            generated = _save_first_image(result, dest)
374	            generated["request_id"] = submission.get("request_id")
375	        results.append({**concept, "generated": generated})
376	    return results
377	
378	
379	def write_grid(results: list[dict[str, Any]], out_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
380	    paths: list[tuple[Path, str]] = []
381	    for item in results:
382	        gen = item.get("generated") or {}
383	        path = Path(str(gen.get("path") or ""))
384	        if path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0:
385	            paths.append((path, str(item.get("name") or item.get("candidate_id"))))
386	    if not paths:
387	        return {"path": None, "image_count": 0, "reason": "no images available"}
388	    try:
389	        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
390	    except ImportError:
391	        return {"path": None, "image_count": len(paths), "reason": "Pillow unavailable"}
392	    cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(len(paths))))
393	    rows = math.ceil(len(paths) / cols)
394	    tile = 384
395	    label_h = 28
396	    grid = Image.new("RGB", (cols * tile, rows * (tile + label_h)), (16, 16, 18))
397	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(grid)
398	    try:
399	        font = ImageFont.truetype("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf", 16)
400	    except OSError:
401	        font = ImageFont.load_default()
402	    for index, (path, label) in enumerate(paths):
403	        col = index % cols
404	        row = index // cols
405	        x = col * tile
406	        y = row * (tile + label_h)
407	        try:
408	            with Image.open(path) as opened:
409	                tile_image = opened.convert("RGB")
410	                tile_image.thumbnail((tile, tile))
411	                offset_x = x + (tile - tile_image.width) // 2
412	                offset_y = y + (tile - tile_image.height) // 2
413	                grid.paste(tile_image, (offset_x, offset_y))
414	        except Exception:
415	            draw.rectangle((x, y, x + tile, y + tile), outline=(60, 60, 64), width=2)
416	        draw.text((x + 8, y + tile + 4), label[:40], fill=(220, 220, 220), font=font)
417	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
418	    grid.save(out_path, quality=90)
419	    return {"path": str(out_path), "image_count": len(paths), "cols": cols, "rows": rows}
420	
421	
422	def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
423	    parser = build_parser()
424	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
425	    _validate_args(parser, args)
426	
427	    layout = build_layout(args.out)
428	    image_size_payload = args.image_size if isinstance(args.image_size, str) else dict(args.image_size)
429	
430	    plan = {
431	        "tool": "logo_ideas",
432	        "version": 1,
433	        "mode": "dry-run" if args.dry_run else "run",
434	        "ideas": args.ideas,
435	        "count": args.count,
436	        "provider": args.provider,
437	        "fal_model_id": PROVIDER_MODEL_IDS[args.provider],
438	        "fireworks_model": args.model,
439	        "image_size": image_size_payload,
440	        "output_format": args.output_format,
441	        "out": str(layout["root"]),
442	    }
443	    write_json(layout["root"] / "logo-plan.json", plan)
444	
445	    if args.dry_run:
446	        concepts = _planned_concepts(args.ideas, args.count)
447	        concepts_payload = {"mode": "dry-run", "raw_response": None, "concepts": concepts}
448	    else:
449	        fireworks_key = _load_env_var("FIREWORKS_API_KEY", args.env_file)
450	        response = call_fireworks_concepts(
451	            ideas=args.ideas,
452	            count=args.count,
453	            model=args.model,
454	            api_key=fireworks_key,
455	        )
456	        concepts = parse_concepts(response, count=args.count)
457	        concepts_payload = {
458	            "mode": "run",
459	            "model": args.model,
460	            "usage": response.get("usage"),
461	            "concepts": concepts,
462	        }
463	    write_json(layout["root"] / "concepts.json", concepts_payload)
464	    write_json(
465	        layout["root"] / "prompts.json",
466	        {
467	            "provider": args.provider,
468	            "fal_model_id": PROVIDER_MODEL_IDS[args.provider],
469	            "image_size": image_size_payload,
470	            "output_format": args.output_format,
471	            "prompts": [
472	                {"candidate_id": c["candidate_id"], "name": c["name"], "prompt": c["prompt"]}
473	                for c in concepts
474	            ],
475	        },
476	    )
477	
478	    fal_key = None if args.dry_run else _load_env_var("FAL_KEY", args.env_file)
479	    results = render_concepts(
480	        concepts=concepts,
481	        layout=layout,
482	        provider=args.provider,
483	        image_size=image_size_payload,
484	        output_format=args.output_format,
485	        fal_key=fal_key,
486	        dry_run=args.dry_run,
487	    )
488	
489	    grid = write_grid(results, layout["root"] / "grid.jpg")
490	    manifest = {
491	        "version": 1,
492	        "mode": plan["mode"],
493	        "ideas": args.ideas,
494	        "count": args.count,
495	        "provider": args.provider,
496	        "fireworks_model": args.model,
497	        "image_size": image_size_payload,
498	        "grid": grid,
499	        "candidates": results,
500	    }
501	    write_json(layout["root"] / "logo-manifest.json", manifest)
502	    write_variant_sidecar(layout["root"], _variant_artifacts_for_logo_ideas(results, run_id=os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_RUN_ID", "").strip()))
503	
504	    print(f"wrote_logo_manifest={layout['root'] / 'logo-manifest.json'}")
505	    if grid.get("path"):
506	        print(f"wrote_grid={grid['path']}")
507	    return 0
508	
509	
510	def _variant_artifacts_for_logo_ideas(results: list[dict[str, Any]], *, run_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
511	    group = hashlib.sha256(f"{run_id}:logo_ideas".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
512	    artifacts = []
513	    for index, item in enumerate(results, start=1):
514	        generated = item.get("generated") or {}
515	        path = generated.get("path")
516	        if not path:
517	            continue
518	        artifacts.append(
519	            {
520	                "path": path,
521	                "role": "variant",
522	                "group": group,
523	                "group_index": index,
524	                "duration": None,
525	                "variant_meta": {
526	                    "candidate_id": item.get("candidate_id"),
527	                    "name": item.get("name"),
528	                    "rationale": item.get("rationale"),
529	                    "prompt": item.get("prompt"),
530	                    "generated": generated,
531	                },
532	            }
533	        )
534	    return artifacts
535	
536	
537	if __name__ == "__main__":
538	    raise SystemExit(main())
539
```

> AGENT

Read it. Here's how the current pipeline actually works:

**Today:**
1. **Kimi K2** → returns JSON with `count` distinct concepts (name + rationale + prompt each)
2. **fal.ai** → submits **one job per concept** (9 separate calls), `num_images=1` each
3. **Pillow** → locally assembles a contact-sheet grid from the 9 saved PNGs

So GPT-image is currently used as a per-image renderer via fal's `openai/gpt-image-2`, called 9 times. The "grid" is a local Pillow composite, not something the model generates.

What you described — *"GPT should generate the grid directly, all images at once"* — is a meaningfully different design. Two ways to read it:

- **(A) One GPT call → one grid image.** Single prompt like "3×3 grid of 9 distinct logo concepts: [brief]", returns one composite image. Cheaper/faster, but you lose individual logo files (can't pluck #4 out cleanly), and composition is at the model's mercy. Kimi could either be dropped or repurposed to draft the single grid-prompt.
- **(B) Keep 9 individual renders, just make gpt-image the default.** Trivial change. You still get 9 separate logo files + the locally-assembled grid.

(A) is what your wording says; (B) is what you'd want if you actually need each logo as its own asset later.

Which one? My instinct is **(B)** — the per-image files are valuable for picking a winner — plus the default flip. But if you really want the single-shot grid for cost/speed, (A) is doable.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py",
  "old_string": "def render_concepts(\n    *,\n    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    layout: dict[str, Path],\n    provider: str,\n    image_size: str | dict[str, int],\n    output_format: str,\n    fal_key: str | None,\n    dry_run: bool,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    ext = _ext_for_format(output_format)\n    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for concept in concepts:\n        candidate_id = concept[\"candidate_id\"]\n        dest = layout[\"images\"] / f\"{candidate_id}.{ext}\"\n        if dry_run or not fal_key:\n            generated = _placeholder_image(dest, concept[\"name\"])\n        else:\n            payload = _fal_payload(provider, concept[\"prompt\"], image_size, output_format)\n            submission = submit_fal_job(provider, payload, fal_key)\n            result = poll_fal_result(submission, fal_key)\n            generated = _save_first_image(result, dest)\n            generated[\"request_id\"] = submission.get(\"request_id\")\n        results.append({**concept, \"generated\": generated})\n    return results",
  "new_string": "def render_concepts(\n    *,\n    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    layout: dict[str, Path],\n    provider: str,\n    image_size: str | dict[str, int],\n    output_format: str,\n    fal_key: str | None,\n    dry_run: bool,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    ext = _ext_for_format(output_format)\n    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for concept in concepts:\n        candidate_id = concept[\"candidate_id\"]\n        dest = layout[\"images\"] / f\"{candidate_id}.{ext}\"\n        if dry_run or not fal_key:\n            generated = _placeholder_image(dest, concept[\"name\"])\n        else:\n            payload = _fal_payload(provider, concept[\"prompt\"], image_size, output_format)\n            submission = submit_fal_job(provider, payload, fal_key)\n            result = poll_fal_result(submission, fal_key)\n            generated = _save_first_image(result, dest)\n            generated[\"request_id\"] = submission.get(\"request_id\")\n        results.append({**concept, \"generated\": generated})\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_grid_prompt(ideas: str, concepts: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:\n    n = len(concepts)\n    cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))\n    rows = math.ceil(n / cols)\n    cells = \"\\n\".join(\n        f\"Cell {i + 1} ({c.get('name') or c['candidate_id']}): {c['prompt']}\"\n        for i, c in enumerate(concepts)\n    )\n    return (\n        f\"A {rows}x{cols} contact-sheet grid containing {n} distinct logo concepts on a \"\n        f\"clean neutral dark background. Each cell shows ONE complete, separate logo at \"\n        f\"equal size, with thin gutters between cells. No labels, captions, or watermark text.\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Brief: {ideas}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Cells (in reading order, left-to-right, top-to-bottom):\\n{cells}\"\n    )\n\n\ndef render_grid_image(\n    *,\n    ideas: str,\n    concepts: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    layout: dict[str, Path],\n    provider: str,\n    image_size: str | dict[str, int],\n    output_format: str,\n    fal_key: str | None,\n    dry_run: bool,\n) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any], str]:\n    ext = _ext_for_format(output_format)\n    grid_path = layout[\"root\"] / f\"grid.{ext}\"\n    grid_prompt = build_grid_prompt(ideas, concepts)\n\n    if dry_run or not fal_key:\n        generated = _placeholder_image(grid_path, \"grid (dry-run)\")\n    else:\n        payload = _fal_payload(provider, grid_prompt, image_size, output_format)\n        submission = submit_fal_job(provider, payload, fal_key)\n        result = poll_fal_result(submission, fal_key)\n        generated = _save_first_image(result, grid_path)\n        generated[\"request_id\"] = submission.get(\"request_id\")\n\n    generated[\"grid_prompt\"] = grid_prompt\n    results = [{**c, \"generated\": dict(generated)} for c in concepts]\n    return results, generated, grid_prompt"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py",
  "old_string": "    fal_key = None if args.dry_run else _load_env_var(\"FAL_KEY\", args.env_file)\n    results = render_concepts(\n        concepts=concepts,\n        layout=layout,\n        provider=args.provider,\n        image_size=image_size_payload,\n        output_format=args.output_format,\n        fal_key=fal_key,\n        dry_run=args.dry_run,\n    )\n\n    grid = write_grid(results, layout[\"root\"] / \"grid.jpg\")\n    manifest = {\n        \"version\": 1,\n        \"mode\": plan[\"mode\"],\n        \"ideas\": args.ideas,\n        \"count\": args.count,\n        \"provider\": args.provider,\n        \"fireworks_model\": args.model,\n        \"image_size\": image_size_payload,\n        \"grid\": grid,\n        \"candidates\": results,\n    }",
  "new_string": "    fal_key = None if args.dry_run else _load_env_var(\"FAL_KEY\", args.env_file)\n    grid_mode = args.provider in GRID_PROVIDERS\n    if grid_mode:\n        results, grid_generated, grid_prompt = render_grid_image(\n            ideas=args.ideas,\n            concepts=concepts,\n            layout=layout,\n            provider=args.provider,\n            image_size=image_size_payload,\n            output_format=args.output_format,\n            fal_key=fal_key,\n            dry_run=args.dry_run,\n        )\n        grid = {\n            \"path\": grid_generated.get(\"path\"),\n            \"image_count\": 1,\n            \"mode\": \"single-call\",\n            \"prompt\": grid_prompt,\n            \"placeholder\": bool(grid_generated.get(\"placeholder\")),\n        }\n    else:\n        results = render_concepts(\n            concepts=concepts,\n            layout=layout,\n            provider=args.provider,\n            image_size=image_size_payload,\n            output_format=args.output_format,\n            fal_key=fal_key,\n            dry_run=args.dry_run,\n        )\n        grid = write_grid(results, layout[\"root\"] / \"grid.jpg\")\n        grid[\"mode\"] = \"composite\"\n\n    manifest = {\n        \"version\": 1,\n        \"mode\": plan[\"mode\"],\n        \"ideas\": args.ideas,\n        \"count\": args.count,\n        \"provider\": args.provider,\n        \"fireworks_model\": args.model,\n        \"image_size\": image_size_payload,\n        \"grid\": grid,\n        \"candidates\": results,\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "def _variant_artifacts_for_grid(\n    grid_generated: dict[str, Any],\n    results: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    *,\n    run_id: str,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    path = grid_generated.get(\"path\")\n    if not path:\n        return []\n    group = hashlib.sha256(f\"{run_id}:logo_ideas_grid\".encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:16]\n    return [\n        {\n            \"path\": path,\n            \"role\": \"variant\",\n            \"group\": group,\n            \"group_index\": 1,\n            \"duration\": None,\n            \"variant_meta\": {\n                \"candidate_id\": \"grid\",\n                \"name\": \"Logo grid\",\n                \"rationale\": \"Single GPT image-2 render of all concepts as one grid.\",\n                \"prompt\": grid_generated.get(\"grid_prompt\"),\n                \"generated\": {k: v for k, v in grid_generated.items() if k != \"grid_prompt\"},\n                \"concepts\": [\n                    {\n                        \"candidate_id\": c.get(\"candidate_id\"),\n                        \"name\": c.get(\"name\"),\n                        \"rationale\": c.get(\"rationale\"),\n                        \"prompt\": c.get(\"prompt\"),\n                    }\n                    for c in results\n                ],\n            },\n        }\n    ]\n\n\ndef _variant_artifacts_for_logo_ideas(results: list[dict[str, Any]], *, run_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:"
}

Default provider is `z-image` (`fal-ai/z-image/turbo`, 1 image per call). Pass `--provider gpt-image` to use `openai/gpt-image-2` instead.\n3. **Grid** — assemble a contact sheet of all renders for review.\n\n## Inputs\n\n- `--ideas` *(required)* — free-form brief: brand, vibe, motifs, constraints.\n- `--count` — number of logos to generate. Default `9`.\n- `--out` *(required)* — output directory. Concepts, prompts, images, and the grid land here.\n- `--provider` — `z-image` (default) or `gpt-image`.",
  "new_string": "# Logo Ideas\n\nGenerate a grid of distinct logo concepts from a single brief.\n\nPipeline:\n1. **Concepts** — call Kimi K2 on Fireworks (`accounts/fireworks/models/kimi-k2p5` by default) to expand the brief into N varied logo prompts.\n2. **Renders** — two modes depending on `--provider`:\n   - `gpt-image` *(default)*: a **single** fal call to `openai/gpt-image-2` whose prompt asks the model to render all N concepts as one contact-sheet grid. The model output IS the grid; no per-concept files are produced.\n   - `z-image`: one fal call to `fal-ai/z-image/turbo` per concept (N images), then a local Pillow contact-sheet is assembled.\n3. **Grid** — `gpt-image` mode writes the GPT render directly as `grid.{ext}`. `z-image` mode assembles `grid.jpg` locally from the N saved PNGs.\n\n## Inputs\n\n- `--ideas` *(required)* — free-form brief: brand, vibe, motifs, constraints.\n- `--count` — number of logos to generate. Default `9`.\n- `--out` *(required)* — output directory. Concepts, prompts, images, and the grid land here.\n- `--provider` — `gpt-image` (default, single-call grid) or `z-image` (per-image + composite)."
}

tool_use Bash
```json
{
  "command": "PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python <<'PY'\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsrc = Path([REDACTED])\nimg = Image.open(src).convert(\"RGB\")\nW, H = img.size\nprint(\"source:\", W, H)\n\ncols = rows = 3\ncw, ch = W // cols, H // rows\n\n# Step 1: crop 9 cells\ncells = []\nfor r in range(rows):\n    for c in range(cols):\n        cell = img.crop((c*cw, r*ch, (c+1)*cw, (r+1)*ch))\n        cells.append(cell)\n\n# Step 2: downsample each to 16x16 (true favicon)\nfavs = [c.resize((16, 16), Image.LANCZOS) for c in cells]\n\n# Step 3: layout — show actual-size row + zoomed-in 3x3 grid\nPAD_BG = (28, 28, 32)\nLABEL = (210, 210, 215)\n\n# Top: row of 9 actual-size 16px favicons with breathing room (like browser tabs)\ntab_w = 80     # tab-ish chip width\ntab_h = 28     # tab-ish chip height\ntab_gap = 6\ntop_pad_y = 36\ntop_w = 9 * tab_w + 8 * tab_gap + 60\ntop_h = top_pad_y + tab_h + 16\n\n# Bottom: 3x3 grid of zoomed (nearest-neighbor) favicons so pixels are visible\nzoom_cell = 144   # 16 * 9\nzoom_gap = 24\nbot_pad_y = 28\nbot_w = 3 * zoom_cell + 2 * zoom_gap + 60\nbot_h = bot_pad_y + 3 * zoom_cell + 2 * zoom_gap + 28\n\nCW = max(top_w, bot_w)\nCH = top_h + bot_h + 24\n\ncanvas = Image.new(\"RGB\", (CW, CH), PAD_BG)\ndraw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)\ntry:\n    font = ImageFont.truetype(\"/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf\", 14)\n    font_sm = ImageFont.truetype(\"/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf\", 11)\nexcept OSError:\n    font = ImageFont.load_default()\n    font_sm = font\n\n# Top row label\ndraw.text((30, 10), \"Actual size (16x16 favicon, on tab-sized chips)\", fill=LABEL, font=font)\n\nx0 = (CW - (9 * tab_w + 8 * tab_gap)) // 2\ny0 = top_pad_y\nfor i, fav in enumerate(favs):\n    tx = x0 + i * (tab_w + tab_gap)\n    # draw faux browser tab background\n    draw.rounded_rectangle((tx, y0, tx + tab_w, y0 + tab_h), radius=6, fill=(48, 48, 54))\n    # paste favicon at left of tab, vertically centered\n    fy = y0 + (tab_h - 16) // 2\n    canvas.paste(fav, (tx + 8, fy))\n    # draw faux page title\n    draw.text((tx + 8 + 16 + 6, fy + 1), f\"Logo {i+1}\", fill=(220, 220, 225), font=font_sm)\n\n# Bottom: zoomed grid\ndraw.text((30, top_h + 4), \"Zoomed 9x (nearest-neighbor) — what each favicon really resolves to\", fill=LABEL, font=font)\nzx0 = (CW - (3 * zoom_cell + 2 * zoom_gap)) // 2\nzy0 = top_h + bot_pad_y\nfor i, fav in enumerate(favs):\n    r, c = divmod(i, 3)\n    bx = zx0 + c * (zoom_cell + zoom_gap)\n    by = zy0 + r * (zoom_cell + zoom_gap)\n    big = fav.resize((zoom_cell, zoom_cell), Image.NEAREST)\n    canvas.paste(big, (bx, by))\n    draw.rectangle((bx-1, by-1, bx+zoom_cell, by+zoom_cell), outline=(70, 70, 78), width=1)\n\nout = src.parent / \"favicons.png\"\ncanvas.save(out, quality=95)\nprint(\"wrote:\", out, canvas.size)\nPY",
  "description": "Render 9 cells as actual-size favicons + zoomed grid"
}

Now answering the metadata question first, then spy run.

## Metadata for one logo_ideas task

The run dir holds a complete provenance trail. For `runs/artagents_logo_v5_grid/`:

| File | What's in it |
|---|---|
| `logo-plan.json` | The request: ideas, count, provider, fal model id (`openai/gpt-image-2`), fireworks model, image_size, output format, mode |
| `concepts.json` | The 9 Kimi concepts — each with `candidate_id`, `name`, `prompt`, `rationale`. Plus Fireworks token usage. |
| `prompts.json` | Slim per-cell list (candidate_id + name + prompt) + provider info |
| `logo-manifest.json` | Full record: every candidate joined with the `generated` block (fal `request_id`, `source_url`, local `path`, bytes, content_type, **and the composite grid_prompt**) |
| `.artagents.variants.json` | Threads sidecar — one variant entry pointing at `grid.png` with all 9 concepts in `variant_meta` |
| `grid.png` | The image itself |

**To your question — "do we have detailed info on which images are associated with which concept (water-promiser etc.)":** Yes per-concept (name/rationale/prompt/candidate_id are preserved for all 9 cells), **but** because grid mode produces one file, all 9 candidates' `generated.path` point at the same `grid.png`. You can't address an individual cell as its own file. If you want per-image metadata trails, `--provider z-image` writes 9 separate PNGs each with their own `request_id` and metadata.

Two minor warts I noticed:
- The full grid_prompt (~2KB) is duplicated 9× inside `logo-manifest.json` (once per candidate). Wasteful but harmless.
- If you wanted "addressable favicons" out of grid mode, we'd need a post-step that slices the grid into 9 named PNGs + per-cell metadata. Not built today.

Honest assessment: the spy framing worked at full size (trench coats, fedoras, hands-in-pockets postures show clearly) but the model rendered them as **neon line-art** instead of solid-filled silhouettes. At 16×16 they collapse to muddy reddish blobs — worse contrast than the original Logo 1 you liked.

The cause: my brief said "neon rim-light around each silhouette" and the model took that to mean "render the whole figure as glowing outlines." For favicon legibility we need **solid fills** with rim-light as a finish, not line-art figures.

{
  "command": "PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python <<'PY'\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsrc = Path([REDACTED])\nimg = Image.open(src).convert(\"RGB\")\nW, H = img.size\ncols = rows = 2\ncw, ch = W // cols, H // rows\nlabels = [\"Anchor\", \"Art Deco\", \"Brutalist\", \"Samurai\"]\n\ncells = [img.crop((c*cw, r*ch, (c+1)*cw, (r+1)*ch)) for r in range(rows) for c in range(cols)]\nfavs = [c.resize((16, 16), Image.LANCZOS) for c in cells]\n\nPAD_BG = (28, 28, 32); LABEL = (210, 210, 215)\ntab_w, tab_h, tab_gap = 110, 28, 10\ntop_pad_y = 36\ntop_w = 4 * tab_w + 3 * tab_gap + 60\ntop_h = top_pad_y + tab_h + 16\nzoom_cell, zoom_gap, bot_pad_y = 220, 28, 28\nbot_w = 4 * zoom_cell + 3 * zoom_gap + 60\nbot_h = bot_pad_y + zoom_cell + 28\nCW, CH = max(top_w, bot_w), top_h + bot_h + 24\ncanvas = Image.new(\"RGB\", (CW, CH), PAD_BG)\ndraw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)\ntry:\n    font = ImageFont.truetype(\"/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf\", 14)\n    font_sm = ImageFont.truetype(\"/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf\", 11)\nexcept OSError:\n    font = font_sm = ImageFont.load_default()\n\ndraw.text((30, 10), \"Actual size (16x16 favicon, on tab-sized chips)\", fill=LABEL, font=font)\nx0 = (CW - (4 * tab_w + 3 * tab_gap)) // 2\ny0 = top_pad_y\nfor i, (fav, lab) in enumerate(zip(favs, labels)):\n    tx = x0 + i * (tab_w + tab_gap)\n    draw.rounded_rectangle((tx, y0, tx + tab_w, y0 + tab_h), radius=6, fill=(48, 48, 54))\n    fy = y0 + (tab_h - 16) // 2\n    canvas.paste(fav, (tx + 8, fy))\n    draw.text((tx + 8 + 16 + 8, fy + 1), lab, fill=(220, 220, 225), font=font_sm)\n\ndraw.text((30, top_h + 4), \"Zoomed ~14x (nearest-neighbor)\", fill=LABEL, font=font)\nzx0 = (CW - (4 * zoom_cell + 3 * zoom_gap)) // 2\nzy0 = top_h + bot_pad_y\nfor i, (fav, lab) in enumerate(zip(favs, labels)):\n    bx = zx0 + i * (zoom_cell + zoom_gap)\n    big = fav.resize((zoom_cell, zoom_cell), Image.NEAREST)\n    canvas.paste(big, (bx, zy0))\n    draw.rectangle((bx-1, zy0-1, bx+zoom_cell, zy0+zoom_cell), outline=(70, 70, 78), width=1)\n    draw.text((bx + 4, zy0 + zoom_cell + 6), lab, fill=(200, 200, 205), font=font_sm)\n\nout = src.parent / \"favicons.png\"\ncanvas.save(out, quality=95)\nprint(\"wrote:\", out)\nPY\nopen /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/runs/artagents_logo_v7_anchor/grid.png /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/runs/artagents_logo_v7_anchor/favicons.png",

---STAGE sprite_sheet---
# Sprite Sheet Executor

Use `builtin.sprite_sheet` to create animation sprite sheets and sliced frame
previews from a subject plus animation description.

Dry-run or inspect first:

```bash
python3 -m artagents executors inspect builtin.sprite_sheet
python3 -m artagents executors run builtin.sprite_sheet --out runs/sprites/wave --input animation=wave --input subject="neon courier" --dry-run
```

Requires image API credentials for generation and `ffmpeg` for slicing/preview
exports.

## Outputs

Expected files include:

- `{out}/sprite_manifest.json`
- `{out}/sprite_sheet.png`
- `{out}/sprite_sheet_alpha.png`
- `{out}/frames/frame_001.png` and sibling frame PNGs
- `{out}/sprite_preview.mp4`
- `{out}/web/sprite_sheet.webp` and web preview outputs when web export is enabled

Safety-edge warnings mean one or more sliced frames touch the configured edge
margin. Treat that as a soft QA warning for prototypes and a prompt/layout retry
signal for production sprites.
---STAGE generate_image---
# Generate Image Executor

Use `builtin.generate_image` when an agent needs bitmap image assets for timelines,
collages, pitch frames, visual treatments, or fallback art packs.

This executor wraps `artagents.executors.generate_image.run` and expects a prompt file. Put one
prompt per line, or provide a JSON/JSONL list accepted by the underlying CLI.

## Commands

Inspect:

```bash
python3 -m artagents executors inspect builtin.generate_image
```

Dry-run:

```bash
python3 -m artagents executors run builtin.generate_image \
  --out runs/example-images \
  --input prompts_file=runs/example-images/prompts.txt \
  --dry-run
```

Run:

```bash
python3 -m artagents executors run builtin.generate_image \
  --out runs/example-images \
  --input prompts_file=runs/example-images/prompts.txt
```

## Outputs

- Images are written under `{out}/images`.
- The generation manifest is written to `{out}/manifest.json`.

## Presets

Pass `--preset <name>` to use a canned prompt and behaviour bundle. Currently:

- `saint-peter-of-banodoco` — onboarding portrait of the maker as Saint Peter
  of Banodoco; opens the rendered image after writing it (use `--no-open` to
  skip). Run directly via the `run.py` entrypoint, since the executor manifest
  command pipes a prompt file rather than a preset:

  ```bash
  python3 -m artagents.executors.generate_image.run \
    --preset saint-peter-of-banodoco \
    --out-dir runs/first-rite/images \
    --manifest runs/first-rite/manifest.json \
    --force
  ```

  Add `--dry-run` to print the planned API call without spending tokens.

## Requirements

Requires `OPENAI_API_KEY` in the environment or a supported local env file.
```

> AGENT

Short answer: **no clean existing tool for both steps.** Status:

- **Chop a cell out of a grid:** No executor for it. Trivial Pillow — we've been doing it inline (~5 lines).
- **Edit/vary an existing image:** No standalone executor. The closest is `sprite_sheet`, which calls OpenAI's `images/edits` endpoint (image + prompt → new image), but it's wired specifically for animation sprite sheets. `generate_image` is text-to-image only.

The OpenAI `images/edits` endpoint is exactly what you'd want — it takes a reference image plus a text prompt and produces variants conditioned on the reference. We just don't have it exposed as a general-purpose executor.

Two paths from here:

1. **Inline it now** — Pillow-crop cell 4 from `grid.png`, then loop over N variant prompts ("hands raised, sparkles", "robe variant A, magic glyphs", etc.) calling `images/edits` with that crop as the reference. Outputs an edits grid. Fastest; ~30 lines of script.
2. **Add a small `image_edit` executor first** — generalised "input image + prompts → variants" tool that any future orchestrator could call. Right shape, but adds a chunk of work before you see results.

My instinct is **(1) now, (2) later if you find yourself reaching for it again.** Want me to do the inline edit-variants on cell 4?

> AGENT

Got it. Design:

**`vary_grid` orchestrator** — iterative grid editor.
- Input: source grid image + cell selection (`"4"`, `"1,2"`, `"4,5"`, `"all"`) + brief + optional count (≤9, default 9)
- Slices source grid → picks selected cells as references → Kimi expands brief into per-cell variation prompts → single OpenAI `images/edits` call (gpt-image-2 accepts multiple reference images) → returns a new grid that itself is fuel for the next iteration.

Building it now as a sibling to `logo_ideas`.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/orchestrators/vary_grid/STAGE.md",
  "content": "# Vary Grid\n\nIterative grid editor. Take an existing grid image (e.g. one produced by `builtin.logo_ideas`), pick one or more cells to use as references, and produce a new grid of variations.\n\nPipeline:\n1. **Slice** — crop the source grid into its constituent cells (auto-detected from the sibling `logo-manifest.json`, or override with `--source-rows`/`--source-cols`).\n2. **Pick** — keep only the cells listed in `--cells` (e.g. `4`, `1,2`, `4,5`, `1-3`, `all`). Selected cells become reference images.\n3. **Concepts** — call Kimi K2 on Fireworks to expand the brief into N distinct variation prompts. Skip with `--no-kimi` to send the brief verbatim.\n4. **Edit** — single call to OpenAI `gpt-image-2` `images/edits`, attaching the reference image(s) and a composite grid prompt asking for an NxN contact-sheet of variations.\n\nThe output is one grid PNG. Slice it again, pick a cell, vary again — that's the loop.\n\n## Inputs\n\n- `--from PATH` *(required)* — path to source grid image.\n- `--cells SPEC` *(required)* — which cells of the source to use as references (`4`, `1,2`, `1-3,5`, `all`).\n- `--ideas TEXT` *(required)* — variation brief.\n- `--out PATH` *(required)* — output directory.\n- `--count N` — number of variants. Default `9`. Max `9` (single image-edits call returns one grid).\n- `--source-rows N`, `--source-cols N` — override grid layout if not auto-detectable.\n- `--size WxH` — gpt-image-2 size. Default `1024x1024`.\n- `--quality` — `low|medium|high|auto`. Default `high`.\n- `--model` — Fireworks chat model id (default Kimi K2).\n- `--no-kimi` — skip the Kimi expansion; the brief becomes a single composite \"N variations of attached\" prompt.\n- `--env-file` — env file with `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` and `OPENAI_API_KEY`.\n- `--dry-run` — write the plan + ref crops, skip both API calls.\n\n## Env\n\n- `OPENAI_API_KEY` — required for the image-edits call.\n- `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` — required for the Kimi step (skip with `--no-kimi`).\n\n## Outputs\n\n```\n{out}/\n  vary-plan.json         # the request\n  refs/ref-001.png ...   # the cell crops sent as references\n  concepts.json          # Kimi's per-cell variation prompts (or null with --no-kimi)\n  prompts.json           # final per-cell prompts handed to gpt-image-2\n  grid.png               # the new grid (single gpt-image-2 render)\n  vary-manifest.json     # full record incl. the composite edit prompt\n```\n"
}
```

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Vary Grid orchestrator: slice an existing grid, pick cells, edit into a new grid via OpenAI gpt-image-2.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport base64\nimport json\nimport math\nimport re\nimport uuid\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Sequence\nfrom urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError\nfrom urllib.request import Request, urlopen\n\nfrom artagents.executors.generate_image.run import _candidate_env_files, _read_env_value\nfrom artagents.orchestrators.logo_ideas.run import (\n    DEFAULT_FIREWORKS_MODEL,\n    FIREWORKS_CHAT_URL,\n    _http_post_json,\n    call_fireworks_concepts,\n    parse_concepts,\n)\n\n\nOPENAI_EDITS_URL = \"https://api.openai.com/v1/images/edits\"\nDEFAULT_OPENAI_MODEL = \"gpt-image-2\"\nDEFAULT_COUNT = 9\nDEFAULT_SIZE = \"1024x1024\"\nDEFAULT_QUALITY = \"high\"\nMAX_COUNT = 9\n\n\ndef _load_env_var(name: str, env_file: Path | None) -> str:\n    import os\n\n    value = os.environ.get(name, \"\").strip()\n    if value:\n        return value\n    tried: list[str] = [f\"{name} environment variable\"]\n    for candidate in _candidate_env_files(env_file):\n        tried.append(str(candidate))\n        if value := _read_env_value(candidate, name):\n            return value\n    raise SystemExit(f\"{name} not found. Tried: {', '.join(tried)}\")\n\n\ndef write_json(path: Path, payload: Any) -> None:\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef parse_cells(spec: str, total: int) -> list[int]:\n    spec = spec.strip()\n    if not spec:\n        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(\"--cells must not be empty\")\n    if spec.lower() == \"all\":\n        return list(range(1, total + 1))\n    picked: list[int] = []\n    for token in spec.split(\",\"):\n        token = token.strip()\n        if not token:\n            continue\n        if \"-\" in token:\n            lo, hi = token.split(\"-\", 1)\n            a, b = int(lo), int(hi)\n            if a < 1 or b < a or b > total:\n                raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f\"--cells range '{token}' out of bounds (1..{total})\")\n            picked.extend(range(a, b + 1))\n        else:\n            n = int(token)\n            if n < 1 or n > total:\n                raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f\"--cells '{n}' out of bounds (1..{total})\")\n            picked.append(n)\n    seen: set[int] = set()\n    unique: list[int] = []\n    for n in picked:\n        if n not in seen:\n            seen.add(n)\n            unique.append(n)\n    if not unique:\n        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(\"--cells parsed to empty list\")\n    return unique\n\n\ndef detect_source_layout(grid_path: Path, override_rows: int | None, override_cols: int | None) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    if override_rows and override_cols:\n        return override_rows, override_cols\n    sibling = grid_path.parent / \"logo-manifest.json\"\n    if not (override_rows or override_cols) and sibling.is_file():\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(sibling.read_text())\n            n = int(data.get(\"count\") or len(data.get(\"candidates\") or []))\n            if n > 0:\n                cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))\n                rows = math.ceil(n / cols)\n                return rows, cols\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    sibling = grid_path.parent / \"vary-manifest.json\"\n    if not (override_rows or override_cols) and sibling.is_file():\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(sibling.read_text())\n            n = int(data.get(\"count\") or 9)\n            if n > 0:\n                cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))\n                rows = math.ceil(n / cols)\n                return rows, cols\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    return (override_rows or 3), (override_cols or 3)\n\n\ndef slice_grid(grid_path: Path, rows: int, cols: int, out_dir: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    from PIL import Image\n\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    img = Image.open(grid_path).convert(\"RGB\")\n    W, H = img.size\n    cw, ch = W // cols, H // rows\n    cells: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for r in range(rows):\n        for c in range(cols):\n            index = r * cols + c + 1\n            cell = img.crop((c * cw, r * ch, (c + 1) * cw, (r + 1) * ch))\n            path = out_dir / f\"cell-{index:03d}.png\"\n            cell.save(path)\n            cells.append({\"index\": index, \"row\": r + 1, \"col\": c + 1, \"path\": str(path)})\n    return cells\n\n\ndef write_refs(cells: list[dict[str, Any]], picks: list[int], refs_dir: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    from PIL import Image\n\n    refs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    refs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    by_index = {c[\"index\"]: c for c in cells}\n    for ref_index, src_index in enumerate(picks, start=1):\n        cell = by_index[src_index]\n        src_path = Path(cell[\"path\"])\n        dest = refs_dir / f\"ref-{ref_index:03d}.png\"\n        Image.open(src_path).convert(\"RGB\").save(dest)\n        refs.append({\n            \"ref_index\": ref_index,\n            \"source_cell_index\": src_index,\n            \"source_row\": cell[\"row\"],\n            \"source_col\": cell[\"col\"],\n            \"path\": str(dest),\n        })\n    return refs\n\n\ndef _system_prompt() -> str:\n    return (\n        \"You are a creative director iterating on an image. The user will provide a brief and \"\n        \"(implicitly) a reference image. Propose distinct, creatively varied modifications. \"\n        \"Each variation should differ in posture, attire, gesture, or specific magical/visual \"\n        \"effect — not minor color tweaks. Keep the reference's core identity (characters, palette, \"\n        \"silhouette style) intact. Return only JSON.\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _user_prompt(ideas: str, count: int, ref_count: int) -> str:\n    return (\n        f\"Brief:\\n{ideas}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"There are {ref_count} reference image(s) attached describing the source archetype. \"\n        f\"Produce exactly {count} distinct variations. Return JSON of the shape:\\n\"\n        '{\"concepts\":[{\"name\":\"short title\",\"rationale\":\"1 sentence why\",'\n        '\"prompt\":\"single self-contained edit instruction, ~25 words, '\n        \"describing how to modify the reference for this cell — posture, attire, hand gesture, \"\n        'magical effect, framing\"}]}\\n'\n        \"The 'prompt' field is fed verbatim to gpt-image-2 — make it concrete and visual. \"\n        \"Do NOT restate the reference; describe only the modification.\"\n    )\n\n\ndef call_kimi_variations(\n    *,\n    ideas: str,\n    count: int,\n    ref_count: int,\n    model: str,\n    api_key: str,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    payload = {\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"messages\": [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": _system_prompt()},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": _user_prompt(ideas, count, ref_count)},\n        ],\n        \"temperature\": 0.9,\n        \"max_tokens\": 4000,\n        \"response_format\": {\"type\": \"json_object\"},\n    }\n    headers = {\"authorization\": f\"Bearer {api_key}\"}\n    try:\n        return _http_post_json(FIREWORKS_CHAT_URL, headers, payload, timeout=180)\n    except HTTPError as exc:\n        detail = exc.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") if exc.fp else \"\"\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Fireworks chat call failed ({exc.code}): {detail}\") from exc\n    except URLError as exc:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Fireworks chat call failed: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef _planned_concepts(ideas: str, count: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    return [\n        {\n            \"candidate_id\": f\"var-{i:03d}\",\n            \"index\": i,\n            \"name\": f\"Planned variation {i}\",\n            \"rationale\": \"Dry-run placeholder.\",\n            \"prompt\": f\"[dry-run] variation {i} on: {ideas}\",\n        }\n        for i in range(1, count + 1)\n    ]\n\n\ndef renumber_concepts(concepts: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    out = []\n    for i, c in enumerate(concepts, start=1):\n        item = dict(c)\n        item[\"candidate_id\"] = f\"var-{i:03d}\"\n        item[\"index\"] = i\n        out.append(item)\n    return out\n\n\ndef build_grid_prompt(ideas: str, concepts: list[dict[str, Any]], ref_count: int) -> str:\n    n = len(concepts)\n    cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))\n    rows = math.ceil(n / cols)\n    cells_text = \"\\n\".join(\n        f\"Cell {i + 1} ({c.get('name') or c['candidate_id']}): {c['prompt']}\"\n        for i, c in enumerate(concepts)\n    )\n    ref_clause = (\n        f\"You are given {ref_count} reference image(s) showing the source archetype. \"\n        if ref_count > 0\n        else \"\"\n    )\n    return (\n        f\"{ref_clause}Render a {rows}x{cols} contact-sheet grid containing {n} distinct variations \"\n        f\"on that archetype. Keep the reference's core identity intact across every cell — same characters, \"\n        f\"same palette, same silhouette style — and only modify what each cell asks for. \"\n        f\"Each cell sits on a clean black background, equal size, thin gutters between cells, no labels.\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Variation brief: {ideas}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Cells (in reading order, left-to-right, top-to-bottom):\\n{cells_text}\"\n    )\n\n\ndef build_no_kimi_prompt(ideas: str, count: int, ref_count: int) -> str:\n    cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(count)))\n    rows = math.ceil(count / cols)\n    ref_clause = (\n        f\"You are given {ref_count} reference image(s) showing the source archetype. \"\n        if ref_count > 0\n        else \"\"\n    )\n    return (\n        f\"{ref_clause}Render a {rows}x{cols} contact-sheet grid containing {count} distinct variations \"\n        f\"on that archetype. Keep the reference's core identity intact across every cell — same \"\n        f\"characters, same palette, same silhouette style. Each cell explores a different variation \"\n        f\"per this brief: {ideas}. Each cell sits on a clean black background, equal size, thin \"\n        f\"gutters between cells, no labels.\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _multipart_field(name: str, value: str, boundary: str) -> bytes:\n    return (\n        f\"--{boundary}\\r\\n\"\n        f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"{name}\"\\r\\n\\r\\n'\n        f\"{value}\\r\\n\"\n    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef _multipart_file(name: str, path: Path, boundary: str, content_type: str = \"image/png\") -> bytes:\n    header = (\n        f\"--{boundary}\\r\\n\"\n        f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"{name}\"; filename=\"{path.name}\"\\r\\n'\n        f\"Content-Type: {content_type}\\r\\n\\r\\n\"\n    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n    return header + path.read_bytes() + b\"\\r\\n\"\n\n\ndef call_openai_edits(\n    *,\n    prompt: str,\n    image_paths: list[Path],\n    api_key: str,\n    model: str,\n    size: str,\n    quality: str,\n    output_format: str,\n    timeout: int = 600,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    boundary = f\"artagents-{uuid.uuid4().hex}\"\n    parts: list[bytes] = []\n    fields = {\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"prompt\": prompt,\n        \"size\": size,\n        \"quality\": quality,\n        \"n\": \"1\",\n        \"output_format\": output_format,\n    }\n    for k, v in fields.items():\n        parts.append(_multipart_field(k, v, boundary))\n    for path in image_paths:\n        parts.append(_multipart_file(\"image\", path, boundary))\n    parts.append(f\"--{boundary}--\\r\\n\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    body = b\"\".join(parts)\n    request = Request(\n        OPENAI_EDITS_URL,\n        data=body,\n        headers={\n            \"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {api_key}\",\n            \"Content-Type\": f\"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}\",\n        },\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n    try:\n        with urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:\n            return json.loads(response.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n    except HTTPError as exc:\n        detail = exc.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") if exc.fp else \"\"\n        raise SystemExit(f\"OpenAI images/edits failed ({exc.code}): {detail}\") from exc\n    except URLError as exc:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"OpenAI images/edits failed: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef write_b64_image(response: dict[str, Any], dest: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    data = response.get(\"data\") or []\n    if not data or not data[0].get(\"b64_json\"):\n        raise SystemExit(f\"OpenAI response missing b64_json: {str(response)[:300]}\")\n    raw = base64.b64decode(data[0][\"b64_json\"])\n    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    dest.write_bytes(raw)\n    return {\n        \"path\": str(dest),\n        \"bytes\": len(raw),\n Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont\n    except ImportError:\n        dest.write_bytes(b\"\")\n        return {\"path\": str(dest), \"placeholder\": True, \"reason\": \"Pillow unavailable\"}\n    image = Image.new(\"RGB\", (768, 768), (24, 24, 28))\n    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)\n    try:\n        font = ImageFont.truetype(\"/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial Bold.ttf\", 28)\n    except OSError:\n        font = ImageFont.load_default()\n    draw.text((24, 24), \"[dry-run]\", fill=(220, 220, 220), font=font)\n    draw.text((24, 72), label[:48], fill=(180, 180, 180), font=font)\n    image.save(dest)\n    return {\"path\": str(dest), \"placeholder\": True, \"reason\": \"dry-run\"}\n\n\ndef _validate_size(size: str) -> str:\n    match = re.fullmatch(r\"([1-9][0-9]*)x([1-9][0-9]*)\", size.strip())\n    if not match:\n        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(\"--size must be WIDTHxHEIGHT (e.g. 1024x1024)\")\n    w, h = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))\n    if w % 16 != 0 or h % 16 != 0:\n        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(\"--size width/height must be multiples of 16 (gpt-image-2)\")\n    return f\"{w}x{h}\"\n\n\ndef build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Iteratively edit grids: slice -> pick cells -> vary into a new grid.\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--from\", dest=\"from_path\", type=Path, required=True, help=\"Source grid image.\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--cells\", required=True, type=str, help=\"Cell selection: '4', '1,2', '1-3,5', or 'all'.\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--ideas\", required=True, help=\"Variation brief.\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--out\", type=Path, required=True, help=\"Output directory.\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--count\", type=int, default=DEFAULT_COUNT, help=f\"Number of variations (1..{MAX_COUNT}, default {DEFAULT_COUNT}).\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--source-rows\", type=int, default=None, ref crops, skip API calls.\")\n    return p\n\n\ndef _validate_args(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser, args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n    if args.count < 1 or args.count > MAX_COUNT:\n End-to-end the tool worked — sliced, picked cell 4, Kimi expanded into 9 variations, prompts assembled. Then hit a wall:

```
OpenAI images/edits failed (400): Billing hard limit has been reached.
```

The OpenAI direct account is capped. The new tool wrote everything *up to* the API call: `source_cells/`, `refs/ref-001.png` (cell 4), `concepts.json`, `prompts.json`, `vary-plan.json`. Just no `grid.png`.

Two options:

1. **Top up the OpenAI hard limit** — your call, billing dashboard. Then the same command runs through.
2. **Re-route `vary_grid` through fal** like `logo_ideas` already does. fal exposes an image-edit endpoint (e.g. `fal-ai/gpt-image-1/edit-image`) that accepts reference image URLs — would need a small refactor (switch from multipart-to-OpenAI to JSON-with-image_urls-to-fal-queue, plus a step to upload refs to fal storage so they get URLs). ~30-60 min of work.

Which do you want? Option 2 is the more durable fix since all the rest of the pipeline already runs through fal anyway.

# GPT Image 2 Edit API Schema

## Input Image Parameter

The parameter is **`image_urls`** (array of strings, required). According to the documentation: `"image_urls` `list<[string](#type-string)>`\* required The URLs of the images to use as a reference for the generation."`

Supported formats include publicly accessible URLs and Base64 data URIs.

## Complete Input Parameters

```json
{
  "prompt": "string (required)",
  "image_urls": ["array of strings (required)"],
  "image_size": "enum or object (default: auto)",
  "quality": "enum (default: high)",
  "num_images": "integer (default: 1)",
  "output_format": "enum (default: png)",
  "sync_mode": "boolean",
  "mask_url": "string"
}
```

## Response Schema

```json
{
  "images": [
    {
      "url": "string (required)",
      "content_type": "string",
      "file_name": "string",
      "file_size": "integer",
      "width": "integer",
      "height": "integer"
    }
  ]
}
```

The specification states: `"images` `list<[ImageFile](#type-ImageFile)>`\* required The generated images."`
```

name=\"{name}\"; filename=\"{path.name}\"\\r\\n'\n        f\"Content-Type: {content_type}\\r\\n\\r\\n\"\n    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n    return header + path.read_bytes() + b\"\\r\\n\"\n\n\ndef call_openai_edits(\n    *,\n    prompt: str,\n    image_paths: list[Path],\n    api_key: str,\n    model: str,\n    size: str,\n    quality: str,\n    output_format: str,\n    timeout: int = 600,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    boundary = f\"artagents-{uuid.uuid4().hex}\"\n    parts: list[bytes] = []\n    fields = {\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"prompt\": prompt,\n        \"size\": size,\n        \"quality\": quality,\n        \"n\": \"1\",\n        \"output_format\": output_format,\n    }\n    for k, v in fields.items():\n        parts.append(_multipart_field(k, v, boundary))\n    for path in image_paths:\n        parts.append(_multipart_file(\"image\", path, boundary))\n    parts.append(f\"--{boundary}--\\r\\n\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    body = b\"\".join(parts)\n    request = Request(\n        OPENAI_EDITS_URL,\n        data=body,\n        headers={\n            \"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {api_key}\",\n            \"Content-Type\": f\"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}\",\n        },\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n    try:\n        with urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:\n            return json.loads(response.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n    except HTTPError as exc:\n        detail = exc.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") if exc.fp else \"\"\n        raise SystemExit(f\"OpenAI images/edits failed ({exc.code}): {detail}\") from exc\n    except URLError as exc:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"OpenAI images/edits failed: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef write_b64_image(response: dict[str, Any], dest: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    data = response.get(\"data\") or []\n    if not data or not data[0].get(\"b64_json\"):\n        raise SystemExit(f\"OpenAI response missing b64_json: {str(response)[:300]}\")\n    raw = base64.b64decode(data[0][\"b64_json\"])\n    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    dest.write_bytes(raw)\n    return {\n        \"path\": str(dest),\n        \"bytes\": len(raw),\n        \"content_type\": \"image/png\",\n    }",
  "new_string": "def _data_uri_for(path: Path) -> str:\n    raw = path.read_bytes()\n    return \"data:image/png;base64,\" + base64.b64encode(raw).decode(\"ascii\")\n\n\ndef _fal_image_size(size: str) -> dict[str, int]:\n    w, h = size.split(\"x\", 1)\n    return {\"width\": int(w), \"height\": int(h)}\n\n\ndef call_fal_edit(\n    *,\n    prompt: str,\n    image_paths: list[Path],\n    api_key: str,\n    size: str,\n    quality: str,\n    output_format: str,\n) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:\n    image_urls = [_data_uri_for(p) for p in image_paths]\n    payload = {\n        \"prompt\": prompt,\n        \"image_urls\": image_urls,\n        \"image_size\": _fal_image_size(size),\n        \"quality\": quality,\n        \"num_images\": 1,\n        \"output_format\": \"jpeg\" if output_format == \"jpg\" else output_format,\n    }\n    submission = submit_fal_job_edit(payload, api_key)\n    result = poll_fal_result(submission, api_key)\n    return submission, result\n\n\ndef submit_fal_job_edit(payload: dict[str, Any], api_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    url = f\"{FAL_QUEUE_URL}/{FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID}\"\n    headers = {\"authorization\": f\"Key {api_key}\"}\n    try:\n        return _http_post_json(url, headers, payload, timeout=120)\n    except HTTPError as exc:\n        detail = exc.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") if exc.fp else \"\"\n        raise SystemExit(f\"fal submit failed ({exc.code}) for {FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID}: {detail}\") from exc\n    except URLError as exc:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"fal submit failed for {FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID}: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef save_fal_image(result: dict[str, Any], dest: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    images = result.get(\"images\") or []\n    if not images:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"fal result had no images: {str(result)[:300]}\")\n    first = images[0]\n    url = first.get(\"url\")\n    if not url:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"fal image entry missing url: {first}\")\n    raw = _http_get_bytes(url, timeout=180)\n    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    dest.write_bytes(raw)\n    return {\n        \"path\": str(dest),\n        \"source_url\": url,\n        \"width\": first.get(\"width\"),\n        \"height\": first.get(\"height\"),\n        \"content_type\": first.get(\"content_type\"),\n        \"bytes\": len(raw),\n    }"
}

> SYSTEM

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user's overarching goal is generating logo concepts iteratively for what appears to be ArtAgents/Banodoco branding. Specific intents in chronological order:
   - Discover and understand the `builtin.logo_ideas` orchestrator
   - Refactor logo_ideas so GPT-Image-2 generates a single composite grid (vs 9 separate fal calls assembled by Pillow), and make gpt-image the default
   - Run iterative logo brainstorms — first "three mythic figures, Daft-Punk neon", then favicon-scale review, then "spy" variants, then anchor-and-vary against Logo 1 of v5
   - Push for stylistic variety beyond neon-on-black, then walk back to "clearly defined colours and simple shapes" with black background
   - Iterate variations on cell 4 (Hooded Operatives) of v9_black_variety with magic gestures, sparkles, robe variants
   - **Build a reusable "vary_grid" tool** that takes a source grid path, a cell selection (`"4"`, `"1,2"`, `"4,5"`, `"all"`), a brief, and an optional count (default 9, max 9), and produces a new grid via image-edit. Output should itself be a grid feedable back into the next iteration.
   - Switch the new tool to route through fal (FAL_KEY working, OpenAI direct hit billing hard limit), keeping the composite-grid prompt architecture (PIL only for slicing input, GPT/fal for grid output).

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - ArtAgents repo structure: `artagents/orchestrators/{name}/` with `orchestrator.yaml`, `run.py`, `STAGE.md`, `__init__.py`
   - Single-call composite-grid prompt pattern: ask gpt-image-2 to render an NxN contact-sheet of N described cells in one image, vs N separate calls
   - Two-step orchestrator pipeline: Kimi K2 (Fireworks) drafts N per-cell prompts → fal/gpt-image-2 renders the composite grid
   - Variant sidecar (`.artagents.variants.json`) for the threads system
   - fal queue API: `https://queue.fal.run/{model_id}` POST then poll status_url/response_url
   - fal `openai/gpt-image-2/edit` accepts `image_urls` as array of URLs OR base64 data URIs (no separate fal storage upload needed)
   - OpenAI `images/edits` multipart upload (used by sprite_sheet but abandoned for vary_grid after billing limit)
   - Cell selection grammar: `"4"`, `"1,2"`, `"1-3,5"`, `"all"`
   - Auto-detect source grid layout from sibling `logo-manifest.json` or `vary-manifest.json`

3. Files and Code Sections:

     - Added new functions:
       ```python
       def build_grid_prompt(ideas: str, concepts: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
           n = len(concepts)
           cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))
           rows = math.ceil(n / cols)
           cells = "\n".join(
               f"Cell {i + 1} ({c.get('name') or c['candidate_id']}): {c['prompt']}"
               for i, c in enumerate(concepts)
           )
           return (
               f"A {rows}x{cols} contact-sheet grid containing {n} distinct logo concepts on a "
               f"clean neutral dark background. Each cell shows ONE complete, separate logo at "
               f"equal size, with thin gutters between cells. No labels, captions, or watermark text.\n\n"
               f"Brief: {ideas}\n\n"
               f"Cells (in reading order, left-to-right, top-to-bottom):\n{cells}"
           )

       def render_grid_image(*, ideas, concepts, layout, provider, image_size, output_format, fal_key, dry_run):
           # single fal call, returns (results, generated, grid_prompt)
       ```
     - In `main()`, added branching: `grid_mode = args.provider in GRID_PROVIDERS` → either `render_grid_image` or original `render_concepts`+`write_grid` path
     - Added `_variant_artifacts_for_grid()` for grid-mode sidecar (single artifact instead of N pointing at same file)

     Key functions:
     - `parse_cells(spec, total) -> list[int]` — parses "4", "1,2", "1-3,5", "all"
     - `detect_source_layout(grid_path, override_rows, override_cols) -> tuple[int, int]` — reads sibling manifest, falls back to 3x3
     - `slice_grid(grid_path, rows, cols, out_dir) -> list[dict]` — Pillow crop into cell-NNN.png files
     - `write_refs(cells, picks, refs_dir) -> list[dict]` — copy selected cells to ref-NNN.png
     - `call_kimi_variations(...)` — Fireworks chat to expand brief into N variation prompts
     - `build_grid_prompt(ideas, concepts, ref_count) -> str` — composite grid prompt mentioning attached refs
     - `build_no_kimi_prompt(ideas, count, ref_count) -> str` — for --no-kimi path
     - `_data_uri_for(path) -> str` — `"data:image/png;base64,..." + base64.b64encode(raw).decode("ascii")`
     - `_fal_image_size(size) -> dict` — splits "WxH" into `{"width": int, "height": int}`
     - `call_fal_edit(...)` — submits with `{"prompt", "image_urls", "image_size", "quality", "num_images": 1, "output_format"}` to `https://queue.fal.run/openai/gpt-image-2/edit`
     - `submit_fal_job_edit(payload, api_key)` — POST to fal queue
     - `save_fal_image(result, dest)` — downloads `result.images[0].url` to disk

> AGENT

Yes — solid coverage of the brief. Each concept has a distinct relational geometry:

- **var-001 Triple Eclipse** — vertical stack, hoods touching
- **var-002 Interwoven Hems** — triangle with merged-hem base
- **var-003 Crossed Sleeves** — back-to-back ring, arms cross to form a star
- **var-004 Shared Mantle** — one robe, three hoods (3→1)
- **var-005 Embracing Triad** — tight group hug
- **var-006 Horizontal Stack** — laid-flat side-by-side
- **var-007 Conjoined Spiral** — coiled column
- **var-008 Overlapping Crescent** — curved arc
- **var-009 Fused Ascension** — single body, three colour-bands

Color identity is preserved per figure across every cell, "fill the frame / overlap" is honoured, and 9 different formal strategies (vertical, lateral, radial, fused, embraced, spiraled). The two I'd flag as risky:

- **var-003** is gesture-heavy — gpt-image often blurs that into a tangle.
- **var-006** lying-down may read as "dead bodies" rather than blended.

But on the whole — good prompts, good rationales, well-distributed across the design space.

38	def _load_env_var(name: str, env_file: Path | None) -> str:
39	    import os
40	
41	    value = os.environ.get(name, "").strip()
42	    if value:
43	        return value
44	    tried: list[str] = [f"{name} environment variable"]
45	    for candidate in _candidate_env_files(env_file):
46	        tried.append(str(candidate))
47	        if value := _read_env_value(candidate, name):
48	            return value
49	    raise SystemExit(f"{name} not found. Tried: {', '.join(tried)}")

52	def write_json(path: Path, payload: Any) -> None:
53	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
54	    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
55	
56	
57	def parse_cells(spec: str, total: int) -> list[int]:
58	    spec = spec.strip()
59	    if not spec:
60	        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("--cells must not be empty")
61	    if spec.lower() == "all":
62	        return list(range(1, total + 1))
63	    picked: list[int] = []
64	    for token in spec.split(","):
65	        token = token.strip()
66	        if not token:
67	            continue
68	        if "-" in token:
69	            lo, hi = token.split("-", 1)
70	            a, b = int(lo), int(hi)
71	            if a < 1 or b < a or b > total:
72	                raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"--cells range '{token}' out of bounds (1..{total})")
73	            picked.extend(range(a, b + 1))
74	        else:
75	            n = int(token)
76	            if n < 1 or n > total:
77	                raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"--cells '{n}' out of bounds (1..{total})")
78	            picked.append(n)
79	    seen: set[int] = set()
80	    unique: list[int] = []
81	    for n in picked:
82	        if n not in seen:
83	            seen.add(n)
84	            unique.append(n)
85	    if not unique:
86	        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("--cells parsed to empty list")
87	    return unique
88
