90	def detect_source_layout(grid_path: Path, override_rows: int | None, override_cols: int | None) -> tuple[int, int]:
91	    if override_rows and override_cols:
92	        return override_rows, override_cols
93	    sibling = grid_path.parent / "logo-manifest.json"
94	    if not (override_rows or override_cols) and sibling.is_file():
95	        try:
96	            data = json.loads(sibling.read_text())
97	            n = int(data.get("count") or len(data.get("candidates") or []))
98	            if n > 0:
99	                cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))
100	                rows = math.ceil(n / cols)
101	                return rows, cols
102	        except Exception:
103	            pass
104	    sibling = grid_path.parent / "vary-manifest.json"
105	    if not (override_rows or override_cols) and sibling.is_file():
106	        try:
107	            data = json.loads(sibling.read_text())
108	            n = int(data.get("count") or 9)
109	            if n > 0:
110	                cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))
111	                rows = math.ceil(n / cols)
112	                return rows, cols
113	        except Exception:
114	            pass
115	    return (override_rows or 3), (override_cols or 3)
116	
117	
118	def slice_grid(grid_path: Path, rows: int, cols: int, out_dir: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
119	    from PIL import Image
120	
121	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
122	    img = Image.open(grid_path).convert("RGB")
123	    W, H = img.size
124	    cw, ch = W // cols, H // rows
125	    cells: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
126	    for r in range(rows):
127	        for c in range(cols):
128	            index = r * cols + c + 1
129	            cell = img.crop((c * cw, r * ch, (c + 1) * cw, (r + 1) * ch))
130	            path = out_dir / f"cell-{index:03d}.png"
131	            cell.save(path)
132	            cells.append({"index": index, "row": r + 1, "col": c + 1, "path": str(path)})
133	    return cells
134	
135	
136	def write_refs(cells: list[dict[str, Any]], picks: list[int], refs_dir: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
137	    from PIL import Image
138	
139	    refs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
140	    refs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
141	    by_index = {c["index"]: c for c in cells}
142	    for ref_index, src_index in enumerate(picks, start=1):
143	        cell = by_index[src_index]
144	        src_path = Path(cell["path"])
145	        dest = refs_dir / f"ref-{ref_index:03d}.png"
146	        Image.open(src_path).convert("RGB").save(dest)
147	        refs.append({
148	            "ref_index": ref_index,
149	            "source_cell_index": src_index,
150	            "source_row": cell["row"],
151	            "source_col": cell["col"],
152	            "path": str(dest),
153	        })
154	    return refs

157	def _system_prompt() -> str:
158	    return (
159	        "You are a creative director iterating on an image. The user will provide a brief and "
160	        "(implicitly) a reference image. Propose distinct, creatively varied modifications. "
161	        "Each variation should differ in posture, attire, gesture, or specific magical/visual "
162	        "effect — not minor color tweaks. Keep the reference's core identity (characters, palette, "
163	        "silhouette style) intact. Return only JSON."
164	    )

166	
167	def _user_prompt(ideas: str, count: int, ref_count: int) -> str:
168	    return (
169	        f"Brief:\n{ideas}\n\n"
170	        f"There are {ref_count} reference image(s) attached describing the source archetype. "
171	        f"Produce exactly {count} distinct variations. Return JSON of the shape:\n"
172	        '{"concepts":[{"name":"short title","rationale":"1 sentence why",'
173	        '"prompt":"single self-contained edit instruction, ~25 words, '
174	        "describing how to modify the reference for this cell — posture, attire, hand gesture, "
175	        'magical effect, framing"}]}\n'
176	        "The 'prompt' field is fed verbatim to gpt-image-2 — make it concrete and visual. "
177	        "Do NOT restate the reference; describe only the modification."
178	    )

180	
181	def call_kimi_variations(
182	    *,
183	    ideas: str,
184	    count: int,
185	    ref_count: int,
186	    model: str,
187	    api_key: str,
188	) -> dict[str, Any]:
189	    payload = {
190	        "model": model,
191	        "messages": [
192	            {"role": "system", "content": _system_prompt()},
193	            {"role": "user", "content": _user_prompt(ideas, count, ref_count)},
194	        ],
195	        "temperature": 0.9,
196	        "max_tokens": 4000,
197	        "response_format": {"type": "json_object"},
198	    }
199	    headers = {"authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"}
200	    try:
201	        return _http_post_json(FIREWORKS_CHAT_URL, headers, payload, timeout=180)
202	    except HTTPError as exc:
203	        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
204	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks chat call failed ({exc.code}): {detail}") from exc
205	    except URLError as exc:
206	        raise SystemExit(f"Fireworks chat call failed: {exc}") from exc

222	def renumber_concepts(concepts: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
223	    out = []
224	    for i, c in enumerate(concepts, start=1):
225	        item = dict(c)
226	        item["candidate_id"] = f"var-{i:03d}"
227	        item["index"] = i
228	        out.append(item)
229	    return out

232	def build_grid_prompt(ideas: str, concepts: list[dict[str, Any]], ref_count: int) -> str:
233	    n = len(concepts)
234	    cols = max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))
235	    rows = math.ceil(n / cols)
236	    cells_text = "\n".join(
237	        f"Cell {i + 1} ({c.get('name') or c['candidate_id']}): {c['prompt']}"
238	        for i, c in enumerate(concepts)
239	    )
240	    ref_clause = (
241	        f"You are given {ref_count} reference image(s) showing the source archetype. "
242	        if ref_count > 0
243	        else ""
244	    )
245	    return (
246	        f"{ref_clause}Render a {rows}x{cols} contact-sheet grid containing {n} distinct variations "
247	        f"on that archetype. Keep the reference's core identity intact across every cell — same characters, "
248	        f"same palette, same silhouette style — and only modify what each cell asks for. "
249	        f"Each cell sits on a clean black background, equal size, thin gutters between cells, no labels.\n\n"
250	        f"Variation brief: {ideas}\n\n"
251	        f"Cells (in reading order, left-to-right, top-to-bottom):\n{cells_text}"
252	    )

272	def _data_uri_for(path: Path) -> str:
273	    raw = path.read_bytes()
274	    return "data:image/png;base64," + base64.b64encode(raw).decode("ascii")
275	
276	
277	def _fal_image_size(size: str) -> dict[str, int]:
278	    w, h = size.split("x", 1)
279	    return {"width": int(w), "height": int(h)}
280	
281	
282	def call_fal_edit(
283	    *,
284	    prompt: str,
285	    image_paths: list[Path],
286	    api_key: str,
287	    size: str,
288	    quality: str,
289	    output_format: str,
290	) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
291	    image_urls = [_data_uri_for(p) for p in image_paths]
292	    payload = {
293	        "prompt": prompt,
294	        "image_urls": image_urls,
295	        "image_size": _fal_image_size(size),
296	        "quality": quality,
297	        "num_images": 1,
298	        "output_format": "jpeg" if output_format == "jpg" else output_format,
299	    }
300	    submission = submit_fal_job_edit(payload, api_key)
301	    result = poll_fal_result(submission, api_key)
302	    return submission, result

304	
305	def submit_fal_job_edit(payload: dict[str, Any], api_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
306	    url = f"{FAL_QUEUE_URL}/{FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID}"
307	    headers = {"authorization": f"Key {api_key}"}
308	    try:
309	        return _http_post_json(url, headers, payload, timeout=120)
310	    except HTTPError as exc:
311	        detail = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if exc.fp else ""
312	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submit failed ({exc.code}) for {FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID}: {detail}") from exc
313	    except URLError as exc:
314	        raise SystemExit(f"fal submit failed for {FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID}: {exc}") from exc
315	
316	
317	def save_fal_image(result: dict[str, Any], dest: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
318	    images = result.get("images") or []
319	    if not images:
320	        raise SystemExit(f"fal result had no images: {str(result)[:300]}")
321	    first = images[0]
322	    url = first.get("url")
323	    if not url:
324	        raise SystemExit(f"fal image entry missing url: {first}")
325	    raw = _http_get_bytes(url, timeout=180)
326	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
327	    dest.write_bytes(raw)
328	    return {
329	        "path": str(dest),
330	        "source_url": url,
331	        "width": first.get("width"),
332	        "height": first.get("height"),
333	        "content_type": first.get("content_type"),
334	        "bytes": len(raw),
335	    }
336	
337	
338	def _placeholder_image(dest: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
339	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
340	    try:
341	        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
342	    except ImportError:
343	        dest.write_bytes(b"")
344	        return {"path": str(dest), "placeholder": True, "reason": "Pillow unavailable"}
345	    image = Image.new("RGB", (768, 768), (24, 24, 28))
346	    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
347	    try:
348	        font = ImageFont.truetype("/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial Bold.ttf", 28)
349	    except OSError:
350	        font = ImageFont.load_default()
351	    draw.text((24, 24), "[dry-run]", fill=(220, 220, 220), font=font)
352	    draw.text((24, 72), label[:48], fill=(180, 180, 180), font=font)
353	    image.save(dest)
354	    return {"path": str(dest), "placeholder": True, "reason": "dry-run"}
355	
356	
357	def _validate_size(size: str) -> str:
358	    match = re.fullmatch(r"([1-9][0-9]*)x([1-9][0-9]*)", size.strip())
359	    if not match:
360	        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("--size must be WIDTHxHEIGHT (e.g. 1024x1024)")
361	    w, h = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
362	    if w % 16 != 0 or h % 16 != 0:
363	        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("--size width/height must be multiples of 16 (gpt-image-2)")
364	    return f"{w}x{h}"
365	
366	
367	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
368	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Iteratively edit grids: slice -> pick cells -> vary into a new grid.")
369	    p.add_argument("--from", dest="from_path", type=Path, required=True, help="Source grid image.")
370	    p.add_argument("--cells", required=True, type=str, help="Cell selection: '4', '1,2', '1-3,5', or 'all'.")
371	    p.add_argument("--ideas", required=True, help="Variation brief.")
372	    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="Output directory.")
373	    p.add_argument("--count", type=int, default=DEFAULT_COUNT, help=f"Number of variations (1..{MAX_COUNT}, default {DEFAULT_COUNT}).")
374	    p.add_argument("--source-rows", type=int, default=None, help="Override source grid rows (default auto-detect).")
375	    p.add_argument("--source-cols", type=int, default=None, help="Override source grid cols (default auto-detect).")
376	    p.add_argument("--size", default=DEFAULT_SIZE, type=_validate_size, help=f"gpt-image-2 size WxH (default {DEFAULT_SIZE}).")
377	    p.add_argument("--quality", default=DEFAULT_QUALITY, choices=("low", "medium", "high", "auto"), help=f"gpt-image-2 quality (default {DEFAULT_QUALITY}).")
378	    p.add_argument("--output-format", default="png", choices=("png", "jpeg", "webp"), help="Output image format.")
379	    p.add_argument("--model", default=DEFAULT_FIREWORKS_MODEL, help="Fireworks chat model id.")
380	    p.add_argument("--no-kimi", action="store_true", help="Skip the Kimi expansion; send the brief verbatim.")
381	    p.add_argument("--env-file", type=Path, help="Env file with FIREWORKS_API_KEY and FAL_KEY.")
382	    p.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Plan + write ref crops, skip API calls.")
383	    return p
384	
385	
386	def _validate_args(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser, args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
387	    if args.count < 1 or args.count > MAX_COUNT:
388	        parser.error(f"--count must be in 1..{MAX_COUNT}")
389	    if not args.from_path.is_file():
390	        parser.error(f"--from path does not exist: {args.from_path}")
391	
392	
393	def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
394	    parser = build_parser()
395	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
396	    _validate_args(parser, args)
397	
398	    out_root = args.out.expanduser().resolve()
399	    out_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
400	    cells_dir = out_root / "source_cells"
401	    refs_dir = out_root / "refs"
402	
403	    rows, cols = detect_source_layout(args.from_path, args.source_rows, args.source_cols)
404	    total_cells = rows * cols
405	    picks = parse_cells(args.cells, total_cells)
406	    cells = slice_grid(args.from_path, rows, cols, cells_dir)
407	    refs = write_refs(cells, picks, refs_dir)
408	
409	    plan = {
410	        "tool": "vary_grid",
411	        "version": 1,
412	        "mode": "dry-run" if args.dry_run else "run",
413	        "from": str(args.from_path.resolve()),
414	        "source_layout": {"rows": rows, "cols": cols, "total": total_cells},
415	        "cells_spec": args.cells,
416	        "picked_cells": picks,
417	        "ref_paths": [r["path"] for r in refs],
418	        "ideas": args.ideas,
419	        "count": args.count,
420	        "size": args.size,
421	        "quality": args.quality,
422	        "output_format": args.output_format,
423	        "fal_edit_model_id": FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID,
424	        "fireworks_model": None if args.no_kimi else args.model,
425	        "out": str(out_root),
426	    }
427	    write_json(out_root / "vary-plan.json", plan)
428	
429	    if args.no_kimi:
430	        concepts: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
431	        concepts_payload = {"mode": "no-kimi", "concepts": []}
432	        grid_prompt = build_no_kimi_prompt(args.ideas, args.count, len(refs))
433	    elif args.dry_run:
434	        concepts = _planned_concepts(args.ideas, args.count)
435	        concepts_payload = {"mode": "dry-run", "concepts": concepts, "raw_response": None}
436	        grid_prompt = build_grid_prompt(args.ideas, concepts, len(refs))
437	    else:
438	        fireworks_key = _load_env_var("FIREWORKS_API_KEY", args.env_file)
439	        response = call_kimi_variations(
440	            ideas=args.ideas,
441	            count=args.count,
442	            ref_count=len(refs),
443	            model=args.model,
444	            api_key=fireworks_key,
445	        )
446	        raw = parse_concepts(response, count=args.count)
447	        concepts = renumber_concepts(raw)
448	        concepts_payload = {
449	            "mode": "run",
450	            "model": args.model,
451	            "usage": response.get("usage"),
452	            "concepts": concepts,
453	        }
454	        grid_prompt = build_grid_prompt(args.ideas, concepts, len(refs))
455	
456	    write_json(out_root / "concepts.json", concepts_payload)
457	    write_json(
458	        out_root / "prompts.json",
459	        {
460	            "fal_edit_model_id": FAL_EDIT_MODEL_ID,
461	            "size": args.size,
462	            "quality": args.quality,
463	            "ref_count": len(refs),
464	            "grid_prompt": grid_prompt,
465	            "concepts": [
466	                {"candidate_id": c["candidate_id"], "name": c.get("name"), "prompt": c["prompt"]}
467	                for c in concepts
468	            ] if concepts else None,
469	        },
470	    )
471	
472	    grid_path = out_root / f"grid.{args.output_format}"
473	    if args.dry_run:
474	        generated = _placeholder_image(grid_path, "vary-grid (dry-run)")
475	    else:
476	        fal_key = _load_env_var("FAL_KEY", args.env_file)
477	        ref_paths = [Path(r["path"]) for r in refs]
478	        submission, result = call_fal_edit(

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/orchestrators/vary_grid/run.py",
  "old_string": "def _placeholder_image(dest: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:",
  "new_string": "def render_favicon_mockup(\n    *,\n    grid_path: Path,\n    rows: int,\n    cols: int,\n    dest: Path,\n    label_prefix: str = \"Logo\",\n    favicon_px: int = 16,\n    zoom: int = 9,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont\n\n    img = Image.open(grid_path).convert(\"RGB\")\n    W, H = img.size\n    cw, ch = W // cols, H // rows\n    cells = [\n        img.crop((c * cw, r * ch, (c + 1) * cw, (r + 1) * ch))\n        for r in range(rows)\n        for c in range(cols)\n    ]\n    n = len(cells)\n\n    try:\n        font = ImageFont.truetype(\"/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf\", 12)\n    except OSError:\n        font = ImageFont.load_default()\n\n    chip_pad_x, chip_pad_y, chip_inner_gap, chip_gap = 8, 6, 6, 8\n    label_widths = [font.getbbox(f\"{label_prefix} {i + 1}\")[2] for i in range(n)]\n    chip_h = max(favicon_px + 2 * chip_pad_y, 24)\n\n    zoom_size = favicon_px * zoom\n    zoom_pad = 24\n    zoom_w = cols * zoom_size + (cols + 1) * zoom_pad\n    zoom_h = rows * zoom_size + (rows + 1) * zoom_pad\n\n    title_h = 24\n    chip_row_h = title_h + chip_h + 16\n    chips_total_w = sum(chip_pad_x + favicon_px + chip_inner_gap + lw + chip_pad_x for lw in label_widths) + chip_gap * (n - 1)\n    canvas_w = max(zoom_w, chips_total_w + 32)\n    canvas_h = chip_row_h + title_h + zoom_h + 16\n    bg = (24, 24, 28)\n    canvas = Image.new(\"RGB\", (canvas_w, canvas_h), bg)\n    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)\n    fg = (220, 220, 220)\n\n    draw.text((16, 4), f\"Actual size ({favicon_px}x{favicon_px} favicon, on tab-sized chips)\", fill=fg, font=font)\n    x, y = 16, title_h\n    for i, crop in enumerate(cells):\n        lw = label_widths[i]\n        cw_chip = chip_pad_x + favicon_px + chip_inner_gap + lw + chip_pad_x\n        draw.rounded_rectangle((x, y, x + cw_chip, y + chip_h), radius=6, fill=(40, 42, 50), outline=(80, 84, 100))\n        fav = crop.resize((favicon_px, favicon_px), Image.LANCZOS)\n        canvas.paste(fav, (x + chip_pad_x, y + (chip_h - favicon_px) // 2))\n        label = f\"{label_prefix} {i + 1}\"\n        lh = font.getbbox(label)[3]\n        draw.text((x + chip_pad_x + favicon_px + chip_inner_gap, y + (chip_h - lh) // 2 - 2), label, fill=fg, font=font)\n        x += cw_chip + chip_gap\n\n    zy = chip_row_h + 4\n    draw.text((16, zy), f\"Zoomed {zoom}x (nearest-neighbor)\", fill=fg, font=font)\n    base_y = zy + title_h\n    for i, crop in enumerate(cells):\n        r, c = divmod(i, cols)\n        zoomed = crop.resize((favicon_px, favicon_px), Image.LANCZOS).resize((zoom_size, zoom_size), Image.NEAREST)\n        px = zoom_pad + c * (zoom_size + zoom_pad)\n        py = base_y + zoom_pad + r * (zoom_size + zoom_pad)\n        draw.rectangle((px - 2, py - 2, px + zoom_size + 2, py + zoom_size + 2), outline=(80, 84, 100))\n        canvas.paste(zoomed, (px, py))\n\n    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    canvas.save(dest)\n    return {\"path\": str(dest), \"rows\": rows, \"cols\": cols, \"favicon_px\": favicon_px, \"zoom\": zoom}\n\n\ndef _placeholder_image(dest: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:"
}
